7.5 SOCCER THREE-WAY AND WINNER-LEAN DISCIPLINE (the draw is a first-class outcome).
- THE BLINDSPOT: winner leans were two-way even in near-equal soccer; China-Thailand (0-0) punished a "Thailand narrowly" lean where the DRAW was the modal outcome. Winner leans across sports also repeatedly missed without consequence-tracking.
- THE RULE: every soccer winner lean must state THREE probabilities (home/draw/away); in near-equal or defensive/wet matchups the draw is often 28-35% and can be the largest single outcome - say so rather than forcing a side. ALL sports: a winner lean under ~55% must be labelled "lean only, near-coinflip"; do not write "narrowly" as a hedge - write the number.
7.6 LINE-AS-EVIDENCE GATE (the offered line is information, not just a target).
- THE BLINDSPOT: lines that contradicted my anchor were treated as wrong instead of informative (NZ NBL 95.5 vs stale 171-H2H; first-10 104.5 encoding a T10 format; Essendon-Carlton backwards orientation caught only by Rule N).
- THE RULE: when the offered line sits MORE THAN ~1.5 scoring units from the GATE 7 projection, write one sentence answering "what does the line-setter know that my anchor does not?" (format, lineup news, venue change, market info). Only fade the line after that sentence names and dismisses the candidate explanations with current sources. A big line-vs-projection gap is a FORMAT/NEWS ALARM first and an edge second.
7.7 LOG-FIRST, COPY-VERBATIM RULE (mechanical fix for chat-vs-log inversions).
- THE BLINDSPOT: three documented cards had the chat answer INVERT the logged ranking (would have flipped 2-2 into 2-4 or vice versa). The cause is re-deriving the ranking while writing the chat answer.
- THE RULE: finalize the ranking ONCE - write the log row first, then copy the ranking VERBATIM into the chat response. Never re-type from memory, never "improve" the order while presenting. If a late fact arrives mid-write, update the LOG first, then re-copy.
7.8 STATMUSE SEASON-SCOPE GUARD (playoffs vs regular season).
- THE BLINDSPOT: "this season" StatMuse queries typically resolve to the REGULAR season; during playoffs/finals the relevant sample is playoff games (different pace, rotations, intensity).
- THE RULE: in any postseason card, query BOTH ("...this season" AND "...in the 2026 playoffs" / "last 5 games"), check the returned table rows are the intended games, and anchor on the playoff/most-recent sample per the recency stack. Never mix regular-season phase averages into a finals phase model without saying so.
7.9 VOID/ABANDONMENT PRE-FLAG (generalized from the KBO rain rule).
- THE RULE: when weather/curfew/light/political risk gives >10-15% chance the match does not reach a normal full state, write a VOID-RISK line pregame naming which legs survive partial completion (completed-phase legs) and which die (full-match legs). Prefer survivable legs for the extreme slots in high void-risk cards - synergy with 7.3 gradeability.
7.10 ACCESS-FLUCTUATION RULE (single fetch failure is not "unavailable").
- THE BLINDSPOT: sources flip between open and blocked day to day (2026-06-10: Cricbuzz and NDTV blocked despite opening the previous day; WNBA.com timing out while ESPN twin page opened instantly).
- THE RULE: before writing UNKNOWN for a decisive fact, walk the fallback chain: alternate domain -> structured API -> reader proxy (Section 8) -> dated search snippets (orientation only). Record which rung worked. Declare UNKNOWN only after the chain is exhausted, and retry blocked primaries next session rather than writing them off permanently.
7.11 AFL LIVE-TOTAL PHASE STABILITY RULE (added 2026-06-12 from Adelaide 121, Western Bulldogs 64).
- THE BLINDSPOT: a low early live scoreboard before Q1 stabilized led to burying Over 169.5, but Adelaide's pressure/territory dominance turned into a 56-point first quarter and a 185 total. Both Under 189.5 and Over 169.5 cashed, making the overlapping band the real edge rather than a one-sided under.
- THE RULE: in AFL live cards, do not use a low early scoreboard snapshot alone to put a moderate over in the bottom slot. Wait for Q1 confirmation or keep overlapping total bands adjacent unless live territory, scoring shots, conversion, weather, and team-quality data all clearly suppress the scoring tail.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 8. LIVE ACCESS TEST MATRIX AND BOT-BLOCK WORKAROUND PLAYBOOK (late 2026-06-10 direct probes + deep-research reconciliation)
------------------------------------------------------------
CORE ACCESS RULES (late 2026-06-10 reconciliation from live probes + deep-research reports):
- HTTP 200 is NOT enough. Classify every fetch as PASS_FRESH, PASS_CONTEXT_ONLY, STALE, BLOCKED, INVALID, or PASS_TRUNCATED (Section 10: the page opened fresh but a summarized fetch cut the body - counts and completeness claims from it are invalid) after checking date, source timestamp, expected body/schema, and block markers (captcha/login/Cloudflare/Access Denied/empty JS shell).
- Exact-date or date-path access is mandatory when available. Generic scoreboards often return source-local "today" rather than AEST today (ESPN MLB/NHL/WNBA examples); capture source-local date/time first, then convert to AEST.
- Official-first does not mean official-blind. Official NBA injury PDFs were current while some NBA CDN scoreboard-style data can be stale; NHL api-web opened in later testing after earlier 403; KBO English pages can redirect to error while Korean official pages open.
- Do not fight blocks. If a site presents CAPTCHA/auth/403/dynamic shell, switch to the next approved source rung instead of re-hammering or bypassing.

VERIFIED OPEN / STRONG WHEN FRESH (re-tested 2026-06-10 from this environment):
- ESPN scoreboards opened for NBA, WNBA, MLB, NHL, AFL, NFL, and soccer competitions tested; exact-date calls opened for the tested sports where used, even when the date had no events. Use ?dates=YYYYMMDD where supported. ESPN rugby-league/NRL endpoints failed.
- MLB Stats API opened for date 06/10/2026 and returned 15 games; use as MLB machine-primary when same-day health check passes, backed by MLB official starting lineups/probables/injury pages.
- Official NBA injury-report landing page opened and discovered current dated PDF; ak-static.cms.nba.com injury PDF opened. WNBA official injury report opened; RotoWire WNBA injury report opened as fallback.
- NHL api-web schedule endpoint opened in later testing; use when fresh, with ESPN NHL as cross-check and game-specific/beat confirmation for goalies.
- AFL injury list opened; AFL/club pages remain primary for teams/late changes.
- Cricbuzz live scores opened and exposed scorecard text; ICC fixtures opened. ESPNcricinfo direct remained 403 but r.jina.ai/https://www.espncricinfo.com/live-cricket-score opened.
- Guardian football and ESPN soccer API opened; TotalCorner schedule opened and remains the explicit-corner source.
- ZeroTackle team lists and injuries opened for NRL. Use club/league-media/late-mail confirmation because NRL.com automation is blocked.
- KBO Korean official schedule opened; English KBO pages may error. NPB BIS English games page opened; use BIS English as first English NPB path.
- Winner League and LKL official pages opened; FIBA LiveStats root 404 is expected because LiveStats is game-specific.
- Open-Meteo opened with hourly Australia/Sydney output; use exact venue coordinates and match-window hours.
- StatMuse opened for sample US-sports stat queries, but deep checks found stale/mis-timed outputs in some query types; use as stats context only after table-date validation.

BLOCKED / THIN / TERTIARY ONLY (late 2026-06-10):
- X.com direct profile/search fetches returned JavaScript shells with "Something went wrong" markers; raw logged-out X pages are not reliable for automated post extraction. Use official X API if automating, official/team page mirrors when available, or search snippets only as discovery. LATE-DAY RETEST: x.com and publish.x.com/oembed returned HTTP 402 hard blocks and syndication.twitter.com was empty/429 - Section 10 supersedes this bullet with the full chain.
- NRL.com draw/team-list/news pages returned CAPTCHA/auth-style barriers; ESPN NRL/rugby-league endpoints failed. Use official club sources, ZeroTackle, Legz, Fox/ABC/major AU media, and credentialed late-mail reporters.
- ESPNcricinfo direct returned 403; use Cricbuzz/ICC/boards first, then r.jina.ai only for public no-login ESPNcricinfo pages when it opens.
- NDTV cricket live scores returned 403 in later testing; keep Sky/CREX/Cricbuzz/CricTracker match-specific pages as alternates.
- Sofascore returned 403 in this environment and Flashscore showed dynamic/login-heavy markers; reports found they can sometimes render usable content. Treat both as tertiary, session-tested cross-checks only, never source of record.
- Footlive connection closed unexpectedly in this environment; Guardian/official/ESPN soccer/TotalCorner are preferred.
- Nitter-style mirrors were unreliable (nitter.net empty; xcancel 403). Do not build process around them.

PROXY / CACHE / MONITORING RULES:
- r.jina.ai is a LAST-RESORT reader for public, no-login pages when direct fetch is blocked. It may cache-miss or fail and does not work on every site. Never use it for login-walled/private/paywalled material, and never let proxied content settle a decisive fact without a second source when a primary exists.
- Wayback Machine can orient on blocked pages, but snapshots can be hours/days old. NEVER settle from an archive timestamp older than the event end or use it for late lineup/injury facts.
- For health checks, log URL, HTTP status, content type, fetched_at, parsed source date, parsed source timestamp, event IDs, and a response/content hash. Alert if a normally fresh source returns stale date, empty shell, schema change, or block marker twice in a row.
- At first use of a critical source each session, treat the first response as an access test; on failure walk the 7.10 fallback chain instead of re-hammering the same URL.
- When multiple primaries fail in one session, or quarterly, re-run this matrix and update this section with the date-stamped results. Access statements in Section 4 carry their test date; newer test results override older claims.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 9. X/TWITTER, REDDIT, AND SOCIAL-SIGNAL VERIFICATION GATE (late-news layer, not source-of-record by default)
------------------------------------------------------------
PURPOSE: Use social sources for facts websites miss or publish late: lineup graphics, late scratches, warmup observations, starting goalies, final 17s/23s, toss and Playing XI, pitch reports, club travel/availability notes, coach comments, import clearances, weather/delay reports, and local-language beat updates.

ACCESS REALITY (tested 2026-06-10):
- Direct x.com profile and search fetches returned large JavaScript shells with "Something went wrong" markers; raw logged-out X access is not a dependable extraction route.
- r.jina.ai can open some public X profile pages, but X search through r.jina.ai was mostly empty/unhelpful. Nitter-style mirrors were unavailable or blocked. Search-engine snippets can discover leads but are SNIPPET only.
- For automated production, use official X API routes and rate limits if credentials/permission exist. Without API access, social checks are manual/browser/search-index discovery and must be labelled accordingly.
- LATE 2026-06-10 RETEST (stricter results override the above): x.com and publish.x.com/oembed returned HTTP 402 hard blocks; syndication.twitter.com returned an empty body via reader and HTTP 429 via direct shell - there is currently NO logged-out X extraction route at all. r.jina.ai opened X profile post TEXT but WITHOUT per-post timestamps: it can confirm a post exists and its wording, never its freshness - grade such facts SNIPPET-equivalent unless the post text itself states the date. Reddit was blocked on EVERY route (www/old/api .json, r.jina.ai proxy, search index, direct shell with browser UA) - treat Reddit as UNAVAILABLE for automation and write "SOCIAL CHECK: reddit unavailable" rather than implying it was checked. The Bluesky public API is a VERIFIED OPEN structured social rung - full playbook in Section 10.

SOURCE TIERS:
- X-OFFICIAL / Tier A: league, team, team PR, league-media, federation, official competition, official club/federation lineup graphics, or organization-affiliated accounts. These can confirm low-ambiguity facts when the post is opened/current and the account identity is linked or obvious.
- X-BEAT / Tier B: credentialed beat reporter or recognized national/local reporter directly reporting a coach/team statement, warmup observation, lineup card, goalie confirmation, late mail, toss/XI, or injury status. Prefer two independent beat/official confirmations for decisive facts.
- X-AGG / Tier C: aggregator accounts reposting other reporting. Use only to discover the original source.
- X-RUMOR / Tier D: fan accounts, blue-check-only accounts, Reddit/forum posts, Discord-style chatter, anonymous tips. Discovery only; cannot anchor #1 or #4 and cannot settle a fact.

VERIFICATION RULES:
- Blue check is not verification. Organizational affiliation, official account linkage, reporter credential/employer history, and original sourcing matter more.
- Social facts must include: account/handle, account tier, post timestamp, fetched/opened timestamp, exact claim, and whether the post is original reporting, official statement, repost, or interpretation.
- A social claim can be OPENED only when the actual post/account page or official API result was opened in the current run. Search-engine snippets are SNIPPET; memory of a reporter's post is ASSUMED.
- For injuries, scratches, minutes/snap/TOI limits, starting goalie/pitcher, cricket XI, soccer XI, AFL/NRL final teams, or import/eligibility clearance: require official confirmation OR two independent high-trust confirmations before it can support #1/#4. If only one beat post exists, label UNKNOWN or PROVISIONAL and cap confidence.
- For NRL and smaller domestic leagues where official websites are blocked/thin, social can be more important but not looser: official club posts, club sites, team-list graphics, broadcaster late mail, and credentialed reporters form the cross-check chain.
- Reddit is radar only. Use it to find links, local reports, or venue chatter, then verify from the original source. Never use Reddit/fan comments as source of record. ACCESS NOTE (late 2026-06-10): every automated Reddit route was blocked in this environment (see Section 10); until a route reopens, Reddit checks are manual-only - state "reddit unavailable" instead of claiming radar coverage.
- Do not use social to infer medical clearance from a training photo, travel photo, warmup appearance, or player self-post. Classify active/available/starting/normal role separately.

SPORT-SPECIFIC SOCIAL USE:
- NBA/WNBA: official injury reports control; use team PR and beat reporters for late scratches, minutes limits, starters, and role changes close to tip.
- MLB: official lineup/probable pages control; use team PR/beat reporters for late scratches, opener/bulk changes, catcher/order changes, weather/roof, and bullpen availability.
- NHL: official game center plus team/warmup reporters for starting goalies, scratches, line combinations, and power-play roles; DailyFaceoff is projection until confirmed.
- NFL: official injury/inactive reports control; use team PR/beat reporters for warmup limitations, snap-count expectations, and late inactive context.
- AFL: AFL/club pages control; use club X and verified reporters for late outs, substitute, managed players, and warmup withdrawals.
- NRL: club posts, team-list graphics, broadcaster late mail, ZeroTackle/Fox/Legz, and verified reporters are mandatory because NRL.com automation is brittle.
- Cricket: official board/ICC/Cricbuzz scorecard controls; use team/board X and credentialed cricket reporters for toss, XI, pitch report, weather delay, replacement, and captain comments.
- Soccer: official competition/club lineup graphics and team sheets control; use club/federation X and credentialed reporters for warmup withdrawals, formation/rotation, and local-language team news.
- KBO/NPB: official league/club pages control; use club accounts and local reporters for game-day lineup, starter changes, injury exits, and roster moves.
- Domestic basketball: official league/game center controls; use club/league accounts and local reporters for import clearance, roster absences, coach comments, and final team sheets.

OUTPUT RULE:
- Every card with a social-dependent fact must show one line: "SOCIAL CHECK: [none found / official opened / beat opened / snippets only / unavailable], controlling account/source, timestamp, and confidence effect." If social access is blocked or unverified and the fact matters, cap confidence and keep it out of #1/#4.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 10. SOCIAL-LAYER ACCESS RETEST AND BLUESKY PLAYBOOK (late 2026-06-10 second probe; newer date-stamped results override older access claims)
------------------------------------------------------------
X/TWITTER - ALL LOGGED-OUT ROUTES FAILED THIS PROBE:
- x.com profile fetch: HTTP 402 hard block (not just a JS shell). publish.x.com/oembed (single-tweet embed JSON): HTTP 402. syndication.twitter.com timeline endpoint: empty body via reader fetch, HTTP 429 via direct shell. Nitter-class mirrors: dead (Section 8).
- r.jina.ai CAN open x.com profile pages and return recent post TEXT, but WITHOUT per-post timestamps. Use it to confirm a post's existence/wording, never its freshness; grade such facts SNIPPET-equivalent unless the post text itself contains the date/time. Do not anchor #1/#4 on an undated proxied post.
- The practical X chain that remains: (1) official site/league/club page that embeds or mirrors the same update -> (2) dated search-index snippets naming the post -> (3) r.jina.ai profile read for wording confirmation -> (4) Bluesky parallel/mirror post (below). Official X API only where credentials/permission exist.

REDDIT - FULLY UNAVAILABLE TO AUTOMATION IN THIS ENVIRONMENT (tested late 2026-06-10):
- Blocked on every route: www.reddit.com/.json (blocked), old.reddit.com/.json (blocked), api.reddit.com (blocked), r.jina.ai proxy (network-security block page), search index (crawler refused: domain inaccessible to user agent), direct shell fetch with browser UA (403).
- Operating rule: do NOT list Reddit as a checked source. When Reddit would have been the radar layer, write "SOCIAL CHECK: reddit unavailable". Re-test occasionally; if a route reopens, Reddit returns to Tier D radar-only per Section 9 - never source of record.

BLUESKY PUBLIC API - NEW VERIFIED SOCIAL RUNG (structured JSON, no key, ISO timestamps; tested late 2026-06-10):
- Endpoints on public.api.bsky.app (no auth):
  * Find accounts: /xrpc/app.bsky.actor.searchActors?q={query}&limit=N - verified: surfaced the official nba.com account, credentialed NBA reporters (The Athletic, The Ringer), and tweet-mirror bots.
  * Profile: /xrpc/app.bsky.actor.getProfile?actor={handle} - verified open.
  * Posts: /xrpc/app.bsky.feed.getAuthorFeed?actor={handle}&limit=N - returns post text + createdAt ISO timestamps. Verified FRESH: official nba.com posts from within ~2 hours of fetch (NBA Finals Game 3 content) on 2026-06-10.
  * Keyword post search (app.bsky.feed.searchPosts) returned 403 without auth - account-level reading only. Workflow: searchActors to find the handle, then getAuthorFeed.
- Tier mapping (Section 9 tiers apply unchanged): a DOMAIN-VERIFIED org handle (the handle IS the domain, e.g. nba.com) is Tier A-equivalent for low-ambiguity facts - Bluesky domain handles are cryptographically tied to the domain and are stronger identity proof than an X blue check. Credentialed reporters = Tier B (confirm employer linkage in the profile). Tweet-mirror/repost bots = Tier C aggregator - discovery only; verify against the original/official source. Fan accounts = Tier D.
- Coverage caveat: Bluesky is deep for US sports media, thin or absent for many leagues (KBO/NPB/cricket boards/AFL-NRL clubs vary). Absence of a Bluesky post is NEVER evidence of anything; Bluesky ADDS a rung, it does not replace official pages.

FETCH-TOOL TRUNCATION TRAP (new fetch classification - affects every large JSON/scoreboard):
- Summarized fetch tools TRUNCATE large bodies before analysis. Demonstrated 2026-06-10: the ESPN MLB scoreboard for 20260610 appeared to contain 6 events via summarized fetch but contained 15 via direct shell fetch (Invoke-RestMethod) - same URL, same minute.
- RULE: never conclude an event count, a full slate, or "fixture missing from schedule" from a summarized fetch of a large JSON. For counting/completeness questions, fetch the JSON in the shell (Invoke-RestMethod/curl) and count programmatically. Summarized fetches remain fine for single-game state questions. Add PASS_TRUNCATED to the Section 8 fetch classifications: an opened, fresh page whose body was cut before analysis - counts and completeness claims from it are INVALID.

SAME-DAY FLUCTUATION CONFIRMATIONS (late 2026-06-10): Cricbuzz was open earlier on 2026-06-10 and BLOCKED late-day; r.jina.ai/ESPNcricinfo live-scores opened in the same late window (full recent results visible, including Bangladesh 284/8 def. Australia 191/9 by 86 runs DLS). MLB statsapi (15 games for 06/10), ESPN exact-date scoreboards, StatMuse (last-10 table current through June 9), and the Bluesky endpoints all confirmed open late-day. This validates Rule 7.10: retry blocked sources each session, walk the chain, and record which rung worked.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 11. RANK-SLOT META-CALIBRATION - FULL-LOG PARSE 2026-06-10 (edges found by counting every graded slot, not just the penalty ledger)
------------------------------------------------------------
METHOD AND HONESTY: programmatic parse of every Results-table row in the prediction log (182 rows; 172 active after removing rows marked historical/non-counting/void; 4 unparseable; verdicts taken in listed rank order). Counts are approximate (a few rows mix live-update and original cards) but the sample is far larger than the penalty ledger and the relative patterns are robust. Recount after every ~20 new graded cards (extends the 6.11 calibration loop).

11.1 RAW SLOT HIT RATES (all sports, all graded slots):
- #1: 107W-58L (64.8%) | #2: 96W-72L (57.1%) | #3: 79W-86L (47.9%) | #4: 82W-84L (49.4%).
- 8-option cards: #5 57.1%, #6 47.8%, #7 52.9%, #8 6W-11L (35.3%).
- RAW vs PENALTY KPI: the "~82.7% top pick" figure is the PENALTY ledger (26 penalties/~150 cards, after exemptions such as the one-goal rule); the RAW #1 hit rate is ~65%. Track BOTH from now on: the raw slot table is the honest calibration; the penalty ledger is the grading economy. Restated raw targets: #1 raw >=65% and rising; #4 raw <=30%.
- HALVES, NOT SLOTS: #1 vs #2 differ by ~8 points; #3 vs #4 are statistically indistinguishable. The ranking currently separates the top HALF from the bottom HALF and nothing finer. Spend the effort making #4 structural (6.5) rather than fine-ordering the middle; this also reinforces the user's top-two review rule - #2 is nearly as load-bearing as #1.
- #8 (35.3%) already outperforms #4 (49.4%) as a fade - consistent with 6.9: on 8-option cards keep the single most impossible item at #8 and do not promote it into the 4-option #4 of the upper stack.

11.2 PER-SPORT SLOT TABLE (graded rows; n<10 is indicative only):
- Basketball (Euro/domestic/NZ/PBA): #1 31W-15L (67%), #4 wins 13W-33L (28%) - the ONLY sport where #4 behaves like a true fade. The process demonstrably works here; keep structural anchors flowing to these cards.
- WNBA: #1 70.6%, #4 wins 35.3% - acceptable; watch phase-pair construction (7.1).
- MLB: #1 76.2% (best top slot) BUT #4 wins 14W-10L (58.3%) - the MLB bottom slot has been WORSE than a coinflip. The buried winners keep being modest overs and run-line cushions (forbidden-#4 items 1-3). MLB GATE: #4 must be structural-class or 3-independent-failure class; if the card is two totals plus two cushions and nothing qualifies, declare FORCED BOTTOM and cap confidence.
- Cricket: #1 60%, #4 wins 17W-13L (56.7%) - second-worst bottom slot; see 11.3 for the directional cause.
- AFL: #1 66.7%, #4 wins 58.3% (n=12) - bottom-slot leak present; apply the same medicine as MLB.
- NBA: #1 4W-4L, #4 wins 5W-3L (n=8) - the NBA playoff Q1/1H complement-pair cards show NO demonstrated edge in EITHER extreme slot. NBA GATE: Q1/1H complement pairs are coinflip-class by default; they may not anchor #1 or #4 unless a counted prior (7.2) >=65% with a confirmed availability/pace mechanism exists; otherwise cap confidence card-wide and put structural items (if any) at the extremes.
- KBO: #1 3W-4L, #4 wins 6W-1L (85.7%, n=7) - the ranking has been near-INVERTED in KBO. KBO HARD GATE (until 10 consecutive KBO cards land #4 raw <=30%): KBO #4 must be structural-class only; no KBO #1 above Medium confidence; re-read the KBO lessons (park/H2H bias, starter form, live contact state) before every KBO card and frequency-count every option per 7.2.
- Soccer: #1 4W-4L (n=8) - weak anchors. Three-way draw discipline (7.5) and corners gradeability (7.3) dominate; soccer cards default to capped confidence unless a structural item exists.

11.3 CRICKET T20 UNDER-BIAS CORRECTION (the single largest directional bias in the log):
- Counted across all graded cricket cards: 13 of 17 cricket #4 winners were buried OVERS (overwhelmingly powerplay overs in the 44.5-60.5 class); 9 of 12 cricket #1 losers were UNDERS (innings and powerplay).
- Diagnosis: the model systematically over-trusts bowling reputation, venue par, and team form as suppression evidence, and under-prices modern T20 powerplay scoring. The venue-specific lessons (Wankhede, Jaipur, Chepauk) did not generalize into a league-wide prior.
- RULE: on any T20 card, a powerplay or innings OVER may occupy #4 ONLY when structural: reduced-overs format, verified same-day wet/seaming pitch report, confirmed top-order outs with weak replacements, or chase-cap logic. "Strong bowling attack", "venue par", or "team in poor form" are NOT sufficient to bury a PP over. Symmetrically, an innings/PP UNDER may take #1 only with a counted current-season prior per 7.2 ("X of the last Y first innings here stayed under Z"), never a narrative.
- Default T20 prior at modern major-league venues: powerplay 50-65, first innings 165-205. A PP over at 50.5-55.5 or an innings over at 175.5-185.5 sits INSIDE the default band - inside-band overs are forbidden-#4 items (6.6 item 1, restated for cricket).

11.4 CARD INTEGRATION: these per-sport gates run inside GATE 9. When the card's sport carries a #4-leak flag above (MLB, Cricket, AFL, KBO, NBA-phase), write one extra line in the card: "SPORT-LEAK CHECK: [sport] #4 historically wins [rate] - candidate #4 is [structural / forced-bottom]". A forced-bottom declaration in a leak-flagged sport also caps card confidence.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 12. REPRODUCIBLE RESEARCH PROCEDURES AND TOOLING SKILLS (2026-06-10; how to re-run the probes and recounts that produced Sections 8, 10, and 11 - so future updates are consistent, not improvised)
------------------------------------------------------------
12.1 ACCESS-PROBE PROCEDURE (run at first use of a critical source each session, when a source misbehaves, and quarterly):
- Probe with BOTH layers when stakes justify it: (1) a summarized fetch asking explicit verification questions ("is this valid JSON; what date/events does it show; is it a shell/captcha/login page; quote the freshest timestamp visible"), and (2) a direct shell fetch for ground truth on counts and schema.
- Classify the result per Section 8 (PASS_FRESH / PASS_CONTEXT_ONLY / STALE / BLOCKED / INVALID / PASS_TRUNCATED) and record: URL, route used (summarized/shell/proxy), HTTP status or block marker, freshness evidence from the body, and the AEST probe timestamp. Newer date-stamped results override older claims in Sections 4/8/10.
- SEARCH-INDEX BLOCKS ARE SEPARATE FROM FETCH BLOCKS: a domain can be refused at the search-crawler level, meaning even SNIPPET discovery is unavailable for it (discovered 2026-06-10 for reddit.com: a domain-restricted search errored "domain not accessible to user agent"). Never assume snippet fallback exists for a domain without testing it once.
- A blocked/denied fetch is an infrastructure fact, not a content fact: switch rungs per 7.10, never re-hammer, and never bypass security controls, CAPTCHAs, or permission denials to force access - blocked means use another source.

12.2 DIRECT SHELL FETCH PATTERNS (the anti-truncation skill for counting, completeness, and schema checks):
- PowerShell pattern: $r = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "<api-url>" -TimeoutSec 30; then count ($r.events.Count) and enumerate names/status/dates programmatically. curl + a JSON parser is the POSIX equivalent.
- Use this route for: slate counts (the 6-of-15 ESPN truncation trap), schedule completeness, "is this fixture missing" claims, exact ID/field extraction (gamePk, event id, startTimeUTC, gameState), and validating a suspicious summarized fetch.
- Run helper scripts with the call operator (& "script.ps1"); do not use execution-policy override flags - they are blocked as security-control overrides in this environment and are unnecessary.
- A browser User-Agent header is acceptable for sites that legitimately serve browsers, but it is not a bypass tool (it did NOT unblock Reddit) - if a site still refuses, it is BLOCKED, move on.
- Bluesky workflow (Section 10): searchActors?q={name} to find the handle -> getAuthorFeed?actor={handle}&limit=N -> read post text + createdAt; createdAt is UTC ISO - convert to AEST before using it as a freshness anchor.

12.3 LOG-RECOUNT PROCEDURE (run every ~20 graded cards per 6.11/11.4; keeps the Section 11 table honest):
- Parse every Results-table row (lines starting "| YYYY-MM-DD |"); split cells on " | "; exclude rows whose outcome contains historical / non-counting / superseded / VOID markers.
- Count picks via the "N." patterns in Ranked Picks; extract verdict tokens from Card Outcome IN LISTED ORDER and map the first N to ranks 1..N; skip rows whose verdict count is below the pick count rather than guessing.
- KNOWN PARSING PITFALLS (each cost accuracy on the first 2026-06-10 pass): verdicts appear in BOTH cases (won/WON, lost/LOST); parenthetical commentary injects FALSE tokens ("LOST (Samsung won by only 1)") - STRIP all parenthetical content BEFORE token extraction, then match case-insensitively.
- Validate the parser on sample rows by eye before trusting aggregates. Report: W-L and percentage per rank; per sport (normalize sport labels to the part before the first "/"); and bottom-slot market composition (over vs under vs side/cushion) - composition is what exposed the 11.3 cricket under-bias.
- Always reconcile the raw recount against the penalty ledger and explain divergences (penalty exemptions, live-update rows, 8-option stacking) instead of silently replacing either number; the raw table and the penalty ledger are different instruments (11.1).

12.4 EVIDENCE STANDARDS FOR UPDATING THIS DOCUMENT (how learnings enter this file without corrupting it):
- Every access claim carries its test date; stricter/newer overrides older, and the superseded bullet gets a pointer to the superseding section rather than silent deletion (audit trail).
- Every quantitative claim carries its method and sample size, and is marked approximate where the parse has known noise; indicative-only labels for n<10.
- Every new rule must name: the failure it fixes (card or counted pattern), the gate it attaches to (Section 1), and the written output it requires in cards - a rule that produces no written output in the card did not happen (upgrade preamble).
- After any edit batch: grep the section headers, the PRECEDENCE line, and the cross-references in one verification pass. Structural corruption of this file is itself a research risk; one edit pass, one verification pass, every time.

2026-06-12 SOCCER WORLD CUP GROUP-OPENER PHASE LEARNING:
- South Korea 2-1 Czechia confirmed that a strong full-game Over 1.5 can still coexist with a 0-0 halftime in near-even group openers. The goals came after halftime through set pieces, substitutions, chasing state, and fatigue. Attach this to Gate 6/Gate 9 for soccer phase cards: when teams are close in quality and the venue/occasion encourages first-half caution, do not rank 1H Over 0.5 above 1H Under 0.5 unless there is first-half-specific mismatch, lineup, press, or defensive-disruption evidence.

2026-06-12 AFL GEELONG-GOLD COAST RETROSPECTIVE:
- Geelong 15.15 (105) defeated Gold Coast 8.12 (60), total 165. The pregame card wrongly ranked SUNS +37.5 #1 and Cats -17.5 #4; both inverted. Attach this to Gate 6/Gate 9 for AFL side-total cards: the low-total underdog-cushion rule applies to genuine compressed games, not automatically to a strong GMHBA Geelong home-favourite setup. Before ranking an away underdog +30 or higher #1 against Geelong at GMHBA, require fresh evidence that the dog can hold territory and score repeatedly: inside-50 share, clearance/contested-ball parity, functioning ball-use personnel, and no major away/venue failure signal. If those stats are UNKNOWN, and the favourite is unbeaten at home with a midfield/run edge while the underdog is poor away or missing defensive/ball-use players, cap the dog-cushion confidence and keep the favourite-cover plus underdog-low-score path live.

PRECEDENCE: this upgrade sits directly under the UNIVERSAL CORE at the top of this file. If any older sport-specific note conflicts with Sections 1-12 here, the stricter/current rule wins, and the conflict should be written in the card. The ACTIVE PRIORITY OVERRIDE at the top of the file points here; the GATE SEQUENCE (Section 1) is the operational entry point for every card, with Section 7's blindspot gates (especially 7.1 extreme-slot pair assignment, 7.2 frequency-count probabilities, and 7.6 line-as-evidence), Section 8's access/freshness tests, Section 9's social verification gate, Section 10's social-access playbook and truncation rule, and Section 11's per-sport slot gates folded into GATES 3, 4, 7, and 9, and Section 12's reproducible procedures governing how Sections 4/8/10/11 are re-tested and recounted over time.

============================================================
2026-06-12 COMPREHENSIVE BLINDSPOT UPGRADE - ROLLING FORM DELTAS, PRESS-CONFERENCE SIGNALS, OFFICIALS SOURCING, NO-API ACCESS REFRESH, AND CROSS-SPORT STAT ADDITIONS (SECTIONS 13-18)
============================================================

WHY THIS UPGRADE (audit snapshot at 2026-06-12):
- The L5/L10/L15/L20 rolling requirement existed in every sport annex but as a COLLECTION list with "(if available, move on)" opt-outs. Nothing forced the windows to be COMPARED against the season average and a historical baseline, nothing defined what a meaningful divergence is, and nothing resolved what to do when L5 and L20 disagree. This upgrade makes the comparison mandatory, written, and verdict-producing.
- Press-conference comments were referenced in nine sport annexes as "tactical-signal inputs, not standalone proof" but had no taxonomy, no directional hypotheses, no weight caps tied to evidence, and NO calibration loop. Whether a quote class actually predicts anything (e.g. a defensive-identity coach publicly demanding more offense) has never been counted. This upgrade builds the counting machinery instead of inventing a base rate.
- Match officials had requirement sections for NFL/NRL/soccer/MLB-umpire but no consolidated, access-tested source map of WHERE assignments publish, WHEN they publish, and what they are allowed to move. This upgrade adds both, with 2026-06-12 access tests.
- These sections attach to the existing Gate sequence (Gates 0-10 of the 2026-06-10 Comprehensive Accuracy Upgrade). They add written outputs; per the upgrade preamble, a rule that produces no written output in the card did not happen.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 13. ROLLING-WINDOW FORM DELTA PROTOCOL (mandatory; attaches to GATE 6 and GATE 7)
------------------------------------------------------------
13.1 THE RULE: for every decision-driving stat on the card (at minimum: scoring/conceding environment for totals, margin-relevant efficiency for sides, the exact phase stat for phase markets, and the prop stat for props), obtain SIX values per team (and per player for props):
   L5 | L10 | L15 | L20 | SEASON AVERAGE | HISTORICAL BASELINE
All six comparisons are REQUIRED for the decision-driving stats. The old "(if available, if not move onto the next)" opt-out is retired for decision-driving stats: a window that cannot be obtained must be listed as MISSING in the STAT COVERAGE line, and a missing L5 or L10 on the decision-driving stat caps card confidence one level. Context stats keep the old opt-out.

13.2 HISTORICAL BASELINE HIERARCHY (use the highest level that opens; tag which level was used):
- H1: same venue / same fixture, last 3 seasons (e.g. team's totals at this ground; H2H at this venue).
- H2: prior full season(s) under the SAME coach and core roster.
- H3: multi-season club average or league-wide baseline for the stat.
Historical values remain CONTEXT under Gate 6 - they never anchor #1. Their job here is the REGIME FLAG (13.5).

13.3 FORM DELTA TABLE (mandatory written output, one row per decision-driving stat):
   STAT | L5 | L10 | L15 | L20 | SEASON | HIST(level) | d5=L5-SEASON | d10=L10-SEASON | dH=SEASON-HIST | VERDICT
VERDICT vocabulary (computed against the sport threshold in 13.4):
- SURGING: d5 and d10 both above +threshold and L5 >= L10 (accelerating).
- IMPROVING: d10 above +threshold.
- STABLE: all window deltas within +-threshold.
- DECLINING: d10 below -threshold.
- COLLAPSING: d5 and d10 both below -threshold and L5 <= L10.
- VOLATILE: d5 exceeds threshold but d10/d15 do not, or adjacent windows alternate sign.

13.4 INITIAL DIVERGENCE THRESHOLDS (operational defaults set near half of typical game-to-game scatter; these are STARTING calibration values, not empirical claims - recalibrate after every 20 graded cards using the ledger):
- NBA/WNBA/Euro basketball team points or opponent points: +-6 (full game), +-3 (half), +-2 (quarter).
- AFL team points: +-12 full game, +-4 per quarter. NRL team points: +-8 full game, +-4 per half.
- MLB runs scored/allowed per game: +-0.8. NHL goals for/against per game: +-0.5.
- Soccer goals for/against per game: +-0.4. Corners per game: +-1.5.
- Cricket T20 innings total: +-15; powerplay: +-6. ODI innings: +-25; first 10 overs: +-8.
- Rate stats (FG%, K%, completion %, run rate): +-1 standard deviation of the team's own game-to-game values where computable, else +-5% relative.

13.5 REGIME FLAG: if |SEASON - HIST| exceeds the threshold, write one sentence naming the most likely cause (coach change, roster turnover, rule/format change, venue/park change, competition tier change). A regime flag means historical baselines and multi-season H2H are DOWNGRADED further for this card - the current-season sample governs, consistent with Gate 6.

13.6 WINDOW-CONFLICT RESOLUTION (when L5 and L20 point opposite ways, decide and WRITE the anchor):
1. Opponent-quality adjust the L5 first: list the five opponents and their tier. A hot L5 against bottom-tier opponents is not a SURGE.
2. Check for an availability/regime change INSIDE the window (star return, trade, coach change, tactical reshuffle): if found, anchor on the post-change subsample regardless of window label.
3. Otherwise the Gate 6 default holds: current-season pace/efficiency and the last 2-3 actual games lead; L15/L20 and historical are context.
4. Mandatory output line: "FORM ANCHOR: [window/subsample] because [opponent-adjustment / regime-change / Gate-6 default]".

13.7 SAMPLE-SIZE GUARD: a 5-game sample of any rate or scoring stat is noise-dominated. An L5 delta may justify the anchor ONLY when paired with a named mechanism (lineup change, schedule spot, tactical shift, confirmed role change). "The number went up" is not a mechanism. L5 alone can never carry #1; this restates Gate 6/Section 6.7(a) in form-table terms.

13.8 FREQUENCY-COUNT INTEGRATION: the form table feeds Section 7.2 directly - the probability for each option starts from the COUNTED hit rate of that exact market across the recency stack (e.g. "over 168.5 hit in 6 of L10 and 4 of opponent L10"), then adjusts for the form-delta verdicts, availability, venue, and conditions with stated reasons.

13.9 US-SPORTS IMPLEMENTATION (StatMuse-first per the existing American-sports rule; verified query shapes, re-tested 2026-06-12):
- "{team} points per game last 5 games" / "...last 10 games" / "...last 15 games" / "...last 20 games" / "...this season"; same pattern for "opponent points per game", "runs per game", "goals per game", "1st quarter points per game", "first half points per game"; players: "{player} points per game last 10 games", "{pitcher} last 5 starts".
- NEW LIVE MISPARSE EXAMPLES (observed 2026-06-12, add to the Misparse Guard): (a) a venue qualifier can be silently dropped - "Pacers record at Milwaukee last 10 games" answered as Pacers last 10 ALL-TIME, ignoring the venue; (b) "Nets record in the last 10 games" resolved to an ALL-TIME last-10 with a stale prior-season side panel. ALWAYS read the answer sentence's restated timeframe AND the table's game dates; if the latest completed game is missing, the page is STALE for L5/L10 use.
- Historical baselines on StatMuse: "{team} points per game in the 2024-25 season" etc.; venue/H2H history via Reference sites when StatMuse misparses.
- Non-US sports: official league stats pages, FIBA LiveStats quarter splits, AFL Tables/Footywire, NRL stats pages via open mirrors, ESPNcricinfo/Cricbuzz series stats via the access chain in Section 16, FBref/soccer league pages. If a window cannot be built from opened sources, it is MISSING - do not estimate it from memory.

13.10 HARD RULE - MANDATORY L5/L10/L15/L20 + H2H + HOME/AWAY COVERAGE, ALL SPORTS (user directive 2026-07-07, elevates and consolidates 13.1-13.9 and the Sports Source Registry StatMuse/Rolling-Baseline gates into one non-optional checklist):
   (a) EVERY card, every sport, must show four things before ranking, not just the scoring-pace form table in 13.3:
       1. Rolling windows L5 / L10 / L15 / L20 for both teams' decision-driving stat(s) (13.1-13.4).
       2. Individual last-game logs (not just averages) for both teams' L5, so a hot/cold average is not hiding one blowout outlier. Also compare each team's immediately previous game because it can reveal rest, travel, bullpen/rotation load, tactical change, injury exits, or a one-game outlier hidden inside the L5 average.
       3. Head-to-head record: most recent meetings (last 3 seasons minimum, or all meetings if fewer exist), scores, and venue.
       4. Home/away split for both teams on the decision-driving stat - a team's overall L10 can be misleading if it is mostly road or home games; separate the split explicitly when the fixture's home/away context matters (nearly always, except neutral-venue tournaments).
   (b) CONFIRMED-LINEUP AND PLAYER/BENCH EXTENSION - added 2026-07-08 from user cleanup directive: once starters, lineups, XIs, team sheets, probable pitchers/goalies, or active lists are confirmed, repeat the same practical L5/L10/L15/L20 and last-game comparison for every player whose role can materially move the listed markets. This includes starting pitchers, goalies, quarterbacks, high-usage scorers, batters in the top/middle order, bowlers expected to bowl powerplay/death overs, set-piece takers, key defenders, and any bench/rotation player with an active outcome role. Do not waste time on nominal bench players who rarely affect the market: for example, cricket bench players outside the Playing XI usually have no direct role and should be marked NOT RELEVANT unless they are substitute/impact-player eligible or a confirmed replacement. If full player windows are too large for the cutoff, cover the decision-driving players first, write PARTIAL, list the missing player group, and cap confidence.
   (c) Any of the four legs or decision-driving player windows that cannot be obtained from an opened source must be written as MISSING with the reason (not silently skipped), and a MISSING decision-driving leg caps card confidence one level, per 13.1.
   (d) This is in addition to, not a replacement for, Gate 6 (recency stack) and Gate 9 (probability table) - the four legs feed those gates, they do not substitute for the projection/ranking work.
   (e) SOURCE PRIORITY - AMERICAN SPORTS LEAGUES (NBA, WNBA, MLB, NFL, NHL, MLS/soccer where covered, CFB, CBB, PGA and similar US/North American leagues): StatMuse is the MANDATORY PRIMARY source for all four legs in (a) and the decision-driving player windows in (b). Try all reasonable StatMuse query avenues before falling back: direct natural-language StatMuse URLs, search-engine discovery snippets, and alternate phrasings such as "{team} last 5 games stats", "{team} last 10 games stats", "{team} last 15 games stats", "{team} last 20 games stats", "{team} last 5 home games", "{team} last 10 away games", "{team} vs {opponent} last games", "{player} last 5 games stats", "{player} last 10 games stats", and role-specific variants like "{pitcher} last 5 starts" or "{player} first quarter points last 10 games". Validate every returned answer sentence, date table, league/season, venue, opponent and phase. Only fall back to a secondary source (Basketball/Baseball/Pro-Football/Hockey-Reference family, Baseball Savant/FanGraphs, official league stat pages/APIs, ESPN site API) when StatMuse fails to open, misparses, omits the target window, is stale, or cannot be validated - and note in the card which StatMuse query failed and which fallback was used. Official sources still control lineups, starters, live state, injuries, availability and settlement; StatMuse never overrides those.
   (f) Non-US sports keep the 13.9 non-US source list for the same four legs (official league pages, FIBA LiveStats, AFL Tables/Footywire, NRL stats mirrors, ESPNcricinfo/Cricbuzz, FBref, KBO/Naver/MyKBO, NPB/SportsNavi, etc.) - StatMuse is not required outside American leagues but may be used as a cross-check if it happens to cover the league/competition.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 14. PRESS-CONFERENCE SIGNAL PROTOCOL (attaches to GATES 4, 5, and 9; extends the existing coach/player press-conference rule with a taxonomy and a calibration loop)
------------------------------------------------------------
14.1 MANDATORY CARD LINE: every serious card carries one line:
"PRESS SIGNAL: [none found / not opened / S-class + speaker + role + date + source + verbatim short quote] -> [hypothesised direction] -> [weight applied]".
If nothing reliable opens, write "PRESS COMMENTS: not found/opened" (existing rule). Quotes must be VERBATIM with source and timestamp; paraphrase-from-memory is ASSUMED-grade and cannot be logged as a signal.

14.2 SIGNAL TAXONOMY (classify every logged quote into exactly one class):
- S1 AVAILABILITY/WORKLOAD: minutes caps, pitch counts, rotation plans, "managed", "we'll see at warmups". FACTUAL class - flows into the Gate 4 availability packet under existing rules; corroborated S1 can move ranks.
- S2 TEMPO/AGGRESSION INTENT: "we need to play faster", "take more shots", "be more expansive", "attack the powerplay". Totals-relevant. Weight: may adjust ADJACENT ranks (#2<->#3, or keep a coinflip pair adjacent) ONLY when corroborated by team selection, confirmed lineup, or recent phase data pointing the same way. Never flips #1/#4 alone.
- S3 IDENTITY CONTRADICTION (the audited case): a coach whose established identity is defensive/control publicly demanding more offense ("we have to make more shots", "we can't win 1-0 every week"), or an attacking coach demanding defensive tightening. HYPOTHESIS A: leading indicator that the staff will trade control for tempo -> mild OVER lean next game. HYPOTHESIS B: public frustration that precedes doubling down on identity or personnel churn -> no effect or UNDER. STATUS: UNVALIDATED - no trustworthy public base rate exists for this signal class in any league. Until the ledger holds >=20 graded S3 samples, S3 may colour the writeup and break a tie between adjacent ranks at most; it may NEVER flip #1/#4, override a counted frequency, or appear as the named mechanism in a #1 stress test.
- S4 ACCOUNTABILITY/CALLOUT: public criticism of players, "unacceptable", "embarrassing". Bounce-back narrative; historically noisy everywhere. Tiebreak colour only.
- S5 CONDITIONS READ: captain/coach pitch read, surface comment, weather prep, roof plans. Weighted when corroborated by a same-day report (this preserves and generalises the existing cricket live-pitch/captain rule, which already outranks venue averages when supported by immediate scoring).
- S6 SMOKESCREEN RISK: playoff/series contexts, injury gamesmanship, lineup misdirection. In any series or finals card, add one sentence of scepticism for any S1-S3 quote and require official-report corroboration before the quote influences anything.

14.3 CALIBRATION LOOP (this is how the "does the quote predict the over?" question gets ANSWERED instead of assumed):
- Every logged signal gets: class, direction hypothesis at card time, and at settlement an OUTCOME (direction confirmed / direction refuted / unresolved).
- The calibration ledger carries these as columns (Section 18). Maintain a per-class running hit rate.
- PROMOTION RULE: a class earns rank-moving weight (beyond adjacent-tiebreak) only at >=20 graded samples AND >=60% directional accuracy; it may influence extreme slots alongside counted frequencies only at >=30 samples and >=65%.
- DEMOTION/INVERSION RULE: a class running <45% over >=20 samples is flagged CONTRARIAN - either invert its use with a written note or retire it. A class hovering 45-60% is NOISE - colour only.
- Per-sport splits: keep the counts split by sport once any sport reaches 10 samples in a class; coach-quote behaviour plausibly differs between leagues, and the KBO/cricket leak history shows sport-level inversions are real.

14.4 SOURCING (cross-reference the Source Registry press-conference section): ASAP Sports verbatim transcripts (re-tested 2026-06-12; current 2026 NBA/college entries confirmed), official club/league sites and YouTube channels (timestamped uploads), AFL.com.au media conferences, MLB.com/team sites, Guardian/BBC/ABC/Fox Sports AU/ESPN bylined presser reports, ESPNcricinfo/Cricbuzz presser articles. Log speaker + role + timestamp; an undated quote cannot be a signal.

14.5 ANTI-OVERFIT GUARD: one spectacular S3 hit does not validate the class - the promotion rule is the only validator. This restates the existing after-a-correct-call overfitting lesson for the press layer.

------------------------------------------------------------
SECTION 15. OFFICIALS / REFEREE / UMPIRE ASSIGNMENT PROTOCOL (attaches to GATE 5; new mandatory card line)
------------------------------------------------------------
15.1 MANDATORY CARD LINE: "OFFICIALS: [names + role + source + timestamp + access tag] / NOT YET ASSIGNED (release is [timing]) / SOURCE UNAVAILABLE (chain walked)". Knowing the release timing makes "not yet posted" distinguishable from "did not check".

15.2 ASSIGNMENT RELEASE TIMING AND SOURCES (access-tested 2026-06-12 unless marked otherwise):
- NBA: official.nba.com "Today's Officials" posts crew chief/referee/umpire per game at ~9:00am ET each game day [OPENED 2026-06-12]. Cross-checks: covers.com NBA referee assignments, refmetrics.com (assignments + tendencies), nbastuffer referee stats.
- MLB: umpire crews rotate one base clockwise per game within a series (HP -> 3B -> 2B -> 1B), so the next HP umpire is predictable from yesterday's crew positions, but CONFIRM day-of via MLB.com Gameday/boxscore or the MLB Stats API live feed officials block once posted. Aggregators: RefMetrics MLB umpire assignments, Action Network MLB umpires page, RotoWire daily umpire stats [all surfaced/OPENED 2026-06-12]. Tendencies: Umpire Scorecards / UmpScores. Postseason crews via MLB.com press releases.
- AFL: the AFL Umpires' Association publishes a per-round "Match Official Appointments" PDF at aflua.com.au (field/boundary/goal umpires per match), typically midweek before the round [OPENED 2026-06-12: Round 1/3/5/6 2026 PDFs confirmed].
- NRL: appointments named with/near the Tuesday team lists; Zero Tackle publishes a weekly "NRL referees, match officials Round N" article and League Unlimited's round teams pages list referee, touch judges, and bunker official per game [both OPENED 2026-06-12]. NRL.com officials pages exist but remain automation-blocked per Section 8 - use the open chain.
- Soccer (Premier League): premierleague.com publishes "Match officials for Matchweek N" (referee, assistants, fourth official, VAR, AVAR) midweek before the matchweek [OPENED 2026-06-12]. Other competitions: competition site announcements; match-centre/lineup pages day-of; Sofascore/Flashscore show the referee when they open (tertiary).
- Cricket: ICC announces umpire/match-referee appointments for international series at icc-cricket.com (match-official appointments page) [OPENED 2026-06-12]; match-day on-field/TV/fourth umpires and match referee appear in the ESPNcricinfo or Cricbuzz Match Info/Officials tab via the Section 16 access chain; franchise leagues list officials on the match centre/scorecard.
- NHL: officials are posted day-of on the official game sheet/game centre; Scouting the Refs publishes daily NHL referee/linesman assignments - KNOWN STANDARD SOURCE, NOT RETESTED THIS PASS; run an access probe on first use.
- NFL: referee crew assignments for each week are tracked publicly (Football Zebras weekly assignments is the standard tracker) - KNOWN STANDARD SOURCE, NOT RETESTED THIS PASS; probe on first use.

15.3 WHAT OFFICIALS MAY MOVE (impact channels, and the cap):
- MLB home-plate umpire: strike-zone size -> K props, BB rate, modest run-environment shift. Quantify ONLY from an opened tendency table (Umpire Scorecards/RotoWire); otherwise list as context.
- NBA/WNBA crew: foul-call rate -> free-throw environment -> totals and FT-dependent props; crew-chief tendency dominates. Same opened-table rule.
- NRL referee: penalty/set-restart (six-again) tempo -> possession volume -> totals lean; bunker strictness -> try-review variance.
- Soccer referee: cards/penalty award rates -> card props, penalty-dependent paths; advantage style -> flow.
- Cricket officials: mostly DRS/over-rate context; totals impact LOW - context only.
- AFL umpiring: free-kick differentials are noisy and crew-level tendency data is thin - context only, never a ranked-pick mechanism.
- HARD CAP: officials are ENVIRONMENT MODIFIERS for totals/props. An official assignment can never be the anchor mechanism for a SIDE, and never moves any pick more than one adjacent rank without an opened, counted tendency table.

SECTION 16. NO-API LIVE-INFORMATION ACCESS PLAYBOOK - 2026-06-12 REFRESH (consolidates Sections 3, 4, 8, 9, 10 into per-fact-type chains; newer date-stamped results override older claims)
------------------------------------------------------------
16.1 PRINCIPLE: every chain below uses only keyless, login-free routes. "No API needed" includes open JSON endpoints that require no key (ESPN site API, MLB Stats API, Open-Meteo, Bluesky public AppView) - these are preferred over rendered pages because they carry machine-readable dates. HTTP 200 is not enough; classify per Section 8 and tag OPENED/SNIPPET/ASSUMED per Section 3.

16.2 FACT-TYPE CHAINS (walk in order; record which rung worked; UNKNOWN only after the chain is exhausted):
- STATS / ROLLING WINDOWS (US sports): StatMuse ask-URLs FIRST (per the American-sports rule; validate table dates; misparse guard per 13.9) -> Reference family (Basketball/Baseball/Pro-Football/Hockey-Reference) -> ESPN site API team/event endpoints -> official league stats.
- STATS (non-US): official league stats/match centre -> FIBA LiveStats / AFL Tables / Footywire / league archives -> ESPN site API where the league exists -> opened previews (context grade).
- STARTING LINEUPS / XI / GOALIES / PITCHERS: official league release per the Section 4 timing windows -> official club page/socials mirror -> RotoWire lineup hubs (confirmation only) -> Bluesky domain-verified org handles -> dated search snippets (SNIPPET). PROJECTED until the sport's official release moment.
- INJURIES/AVAILABILITY: official injury report page/PDF -> club PR -> credentialed beat reporting (opened, dated) -> aggregators (covers/CBS/RotoWire) -> SOCIAL CHECK line.
- OFFICIALS: Section 15.2 chains.
- PRESS CONFERENCES: ASAP Sports -> official club/league video+site -> bylined Guardian/BBC/ABC/Fox/ESPN/cricinfo reports -> nothing opened = "PRESS COMMENTS: not found/opened".
- WEATHER: Open-Meteo hourly JSON for venue lat/lon + BOM/WillyWeather (AU), NWS (US), Met Office (UK); always venue-local match window per the weather-game-window rule.
- SETTLEMENT/FINALS: two independent opened sources; phase legs need explicit period/over splits (Rule X).

16.3 X / TWITTER / REDDIT / ESPN DIRECTIVE RECONCILIATION (user directive 2026-06-12: X/Twitter, Reddit, and ESPN are REQUIRED CHECK TARGETS for match officials, starting lineups, and injuries):
- ESPN: WORKING rung - site pages and the keyless site.api.espn.com scoreboard/summary endpoints (re-confirmed open from a second environment 2026-06-12 for NBA pages; full per-league matrix per Section 8 tests of 2026-06-10). Use exact-date calls; beware summarized-fetch truncation (Section 10).
- X/Twitter: REQUIRED CHECK, executed through the only routes that exist logged-out, in this order: (1) official site/league/club page mirroring the same update, (2) dated search-index snippets naming the post, (3) r.jina.ai profile read for wording confirmation (timestamp-less = SNIPPET grade), (4) Bluesky parallel/mirror post via public.api.bsky.app. Direct x.com remains hard-blocked (HTTP 402, Section 10). The card's mandatory SOCIAL CHECK line states which rung worked. NEVER claim X was checked without an opened rung, and never let a timestamp-less X read settle a decisive fact.
- Reddit: REQUIRED CHECK in intent, but every automated route is blocked in this environment (Section 10 retest). Standing output: "SOCIAL CHECK: reddit unavailable". Re-probe occasionally; if a route reopens, Reddit returns as Tier D radar only - never source of record. Do not list Reddit as checked when it was not opened.
- This reconciliation satisfies the directive honestly: the targets are mandatory CHECKS with documented access states, not mandatory CLAIMS of coverage.

16.4 STATMUSE ENFORCEMENT: see Canonical Hard Data Rule A and Section 13.10. No separate rule is active here.

SECTION 17. CROSS-SPORT STAT BLINDSPOT ADDITIONS (each item names the annex/section it extends; per the rule-cleanup policy, none of these duplicates an existing universal rule)
------------------------------------------------------------
- NBA/WNBA/basketball (extends ROLLING TEAM REQUIREMENTS + SCHEDULE sections): add (a) schedule-density splits - 3-games-in-4-nights and 5-in-7 performance, not just B2B; (b) opponent three-point percentage allowed as a LUCK stat - extreme opponent 3P% over L5-L10 regresses, flag before trusting a defensive surge/collapse; (c) free-throw-rate environment (own + opponent FT rate) as a totals input alongside pace; (d) altitude venues (Denver/Utah class) as a fatigue/totals context for visitors on dense schedules; (e) late-season star minutes-management risk for clinched/eliminated teams as an availability-adjacent flag.
- MLB (extends STARTING PITCHER + LINEUP sections): add (a) times-through-order penalty - a starter's 3rd-time-through wOBA gap is a bullpen-entry timing input for totals and F5 markets; (b) getaway-day / day-game-after-night-game lineup-rest risk - check the posted lineup before trusting season offense; (c) series game number context (sweep-avoidance bullpen usage compounding the existing fatigue rule).
- NHL (extends GOALIE + SHOOTING sections): add team PDO (SV% + SH%) over L10-L20 as an explicit regression flag in the form table - PDO far above/below 1.000 marks unsustainable runs; pairs with the existing GSAx regression rule.
- AFL (extends VENUE AND WEATHER + TEAM SELECTION sections): add (a) venue-dimension differentials (narrow/short grounds vs MCG class) as a totals/style input when teams travel; (b) substitute usage pattern (tactical vs medical sub history) as a late-game scoring context; (c) codify the 6-day-break high-speed-running decline (already in the skill reference) as a form-table adjustment.
- NRL (extends REFEREE/OFFICIATING + POSSESSION sections): add set-restart (six-again) differential and referee penalty tempo per Section 15.3 as a possession-volume totals input.
- Cricket (extends VENUE AND PITCH + WEATHER sections): add (a) a DEW module for night games - dew probability shifts chase preference, death-overs bowling control, and innings-2 run rate; check toss decision against dew expectation; (b) BALL TYPE (Kookaburra/Dukes/SG) swing-and-seam longevity differences when modelling new-ball phases across countries; (c) boundary-dimension capture as a standing field (generalising the Wankhede/Jaipur/Ekana lessons rather than re-learning per venue); (d) over-rate penalty state (fielding restrictions for slow over rate) as a death-overs scoring context where playing conditions apply it.
- Cricket T20 pitch-report classification correction (England Women vs Sri Lanka Women, 2026-06-13 AEST): do not label a surface "road-like" from captain comments or generic dry/sunny notes. Separate (1) actual pitch report, (2) toss/captain comments, and (3) live surface behaviour. In this case the opened pitch-report wording was firm/well-rounded, possible early swing, extra bounce for batters, and possible spin later; Sri Lanka's toss comment was only about unknown conditions/rain yesterday. The road-like label became a live-behaviour inference only after England reached 80+/0 by 8 overs and 100+/0 by 10 overs. Once live evidence reaches that threshold with wickets in hand, abandon any innings-under anchor immediately, but record it as LIVE EVIDENCE, not as the original pitch report.
- Soccer (extends LINEUPS + REFEREE sections): add (a) a rotation-risk calendar flag - league game sandwiched by continental/cup midweeks demands a projected-XI rotation check; (b) manager-change bounce window (first 2-4 games under a new manager) as a regime flag feeding 13.5; (c) referee card/penalty rates per Section 15.3 for card/penalty props only.
- ALL SPORTS (extends MATCH CONTEXT): coach-tenure regime check - a coaching change inside the L20 window invalidates pre-change form rows for anchor purposes (13.6 step 2) and triggers the 13.5 regime sentence.

SECTION 18. LEDGER/LOG SCHEMA EXTENSION AND KPI ADDITIONS (attaches to Sections 19-20 calibration tracking and the results-log template)

18.1 CALIBRATION LEDGER NEW COLUMNS (appended; see SPORTS_CALIBRATION_LEDGER_v2.csv header): form_delta_verdict; form_anchor_window; press_signal_class; press_signal_quote_logged; press_signal_direction_hypothesis; press_signal_outcome; officials_named; officials_source_timestamp; officials_impact_note; source_confidence_score; source_disagreement_note; similar_condition_form; role_replacement_bench_verdict; style_fit_note; volatility_stability_note; prediction_triggers.

18.3 NEW KPIs ON EVERY SETTLEMENT SWEEP (alongside top hit rate / bottom win rate / bucket calibration): (a) press-signal directional hit rate per class (promotion/demotion per 14.3); (b) form-delta verdict usefulness - of cards where the verdict was SURGING/COLLAPSING, how often did the implied direction land; (c) officials-line coverage - share of serious cards carrying a resolved OFFICIALS line; (d) stat-coverage completeness - share of cards with all four windows obtained on the decision-driving stat.
18.4 THRESHOLD RECALIBRATION: every 20 graded cards, revisit the 13.4 thresholds per sport - widen any threshold whose VOLATILE rate exceeds ~40% of cards, tighten any whose STABLE rate exceeds ~80%.

Where an older sport-specific note conflicts, the stricter/current rule wins and the conflict is written in the card. Nothing here relaxes any Gate 0-10 requirement, the forbidden-#4 list, the per-sport leak gates of Section 11, or the social verification gate of Sections 9-10.
